Figure 34
.
Incrementalizing transformation from
\lambda_{\mathsf{inc}}
to
\lambda_{\mathsf{core}}
on terms.
It satisfies the invariant that
\Gamma\vdash t:\tau
in
\lambda_{\mathsf{inc}}
implies
\mathcal{I}\!\left\{\Gamma\right\}\vdash\mathcal{I}_{\Gamma}\!\left\{t\right\}:\mathcal{I}\!\left\{\tau\right\}\times\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma));\Delta(\tau))
in
\lambda_{\mathsf{core}}
.

\begin{aligned} &\mathcal{I}_{\Gamma}\!\left\{\begin{aligned} &\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} for}}}\;x:\tau\;\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} in}}}\;\mathit{xs}\;\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} with}}}\;z:=s:\rho\\
&\;\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} using}}}\;(v_{1}:=y_{1}[u_{1}]:\theta_{1},\dots,\\
&\hskip 8.50012ptv_{n}:=y_{n}[u_{n}]:\theta_{n})\;\{\;t:\tau^{\prime}\times\rho\;\}\end{aligned}\right\}=\\
&(\mathsf{xs},\mathsf{xsUpd}):=\mathcal{I}\!\left\{\mathit{xs}\right\};\\
&(\mathsf{z},\mathsf{seedUpd}):=\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\};\\
&\mathsf{seeds}:=[\mathsf{z}];\,\mathsf{results}:=[\,];\,\mathsf{updaters}:=[\,];\\
&\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{\mathsf{j}}:=[\,]\text{ for }\mathsf{j}=1..n;\\
&\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{\mathsf{j}}:=\emptyset\text{ for }\mathsf{j}=1..n;\\
&\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}:=\emptyset\text{ for }\mathsf{j}=1..n;\\
&\textbf{for }\mathsf{i}\textbf{ from }1\textbf{ to }\mathsf{length}(\mathsf{xs})\textbf{ do}\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{x}:=\mathsf{xs}[\mathsf{i}];\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{for }\mathsf{j}\textbf{ from }1\textbf{ to }n\textbf{ do}\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt(\mathsf{idx}_{\mathsf{j}},\mathsf{idxUpd}_{\mathsf{j}}):=\mathcal{I}\!\left\{u_{\mathsf{j}}\right\};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt(\mathsf{collections}_{\mathsf{j}},\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{\mathsf{j}}):=\mathcal{I}\!\left\{y_{\mathsf{j}}\right\};\\
&\hskip 17.00024ptv_{\mathsf{j}}:=\mathsf{collections}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{idx}_{\mathsf{j}}];\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{push}(\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{\mathsf{j}},\mathsf{idxUpd}_{\mathsf{j}});\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{i}]:=\mathsf{idx}_{\mathsf{j}};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{idx}_{\mathsf{j}}]:=\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{idx}_{\mathsf{j}}]\cup\{\mathsf{i}\};\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt((\mathsf{y},\mathsf{z}^{\prime}),\mathsf{upd}):=\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\};\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{push}(\mathsf{seeds},\mathsf{z}^{\prime});\;\mathsf{push}(\mathsf{results},\mathsf{y});\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{push}(\mathsf{updaters},\mathsf{upd});\;\mathsf{z}:=\mathsf{z}^{\prime};\\
&\mathsf{cache}:=\{\textnormal{inputs:}\,\mathsf{xs},\textnormal{xsUpd:}\,\mathsf{xsUpd},\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{seeds:}\,\mathsf{seeds},\textnormal{seedUpd:}\,\mathsf{seedUpd},\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{collections:}\,\mathsf{collections},\textnormal{collectionUpds:}\,\mathsf{collectionUpds},\\

&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{indicesToIters:}\,\mathsf{indicesToIters},\textnormal{updaters:}\,\mathsf{updaters}\};\\
&(\mathsf{results},\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} mkUpd}}}(\mathsf{cache};\mathsf{update}))\end{aligned}

\begin{aligned} &\mathsf{CacheType}=\{\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{inputs}:\mathsf{List}\;\tau,\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{xsUpd}:\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma));\Delta(\mathsf{List}\;\tau)),\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{seeds}:\mathsf{List}\;\rho,\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{seedUpd}:\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma));\Delta(\rho)),\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{collections}:\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{1}\times\dots\times\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{n},\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{collectionUpds}:\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma));\Delta(\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{1}))\times\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\cdots\times\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma));\Delta(\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{n})),\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{itersToIndices}:\mathsf{Map}\;\mathbb{N}\;\mathsf{Idx}_{1}\times\cdots\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\times\mathsf{Map}\;\mathbb{N}\;\mathsf{Idx}_{n},\\

&\hskip 17.00024pt\cdots\times\mathsf{List}\;\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma_{\mathit{lkp}}));\Delta(\mathsf{Idx}_{n})),\\

&\hskip 17.00024pt\times\mathsf{Map}\;\mathsf{Idx}_{n}\;(\mathsf{Set}\;\mathbb{N}),\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textnormal{updaters}:\mathsf{List}\;\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}));\Delta(\tau^{\prime}\times\rho))\\
&\}\end{aligned}

where each

\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{j}
is a list or name map;
\mathsf{Idx}_{j}
is
\mathbb{N}
or
\mathsf{Name}
accordingly;
\Gamma_{\mathit{lkp}}=\Gamma,x{:}\tau,z{:}\rho
;
\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}=\Gamma_{\mathit{lkp}},v_{1}{:}\theta_{1},\dots,v_{n}{:}\theta_{n}
.

\begin{aligned} &\textbf{{update}}\;(d\gamma:\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)),\ \mathsf{cache}:\mathsf{CacheType}):=\\
&\mathsf{inputs}:=\mathsf{cache}.\textnormal{inputs};\dots;\mathsf{updaters}:=\mathsf{cache}.\textnormal{updaters};\\
&(\mathsf{dxs},\mathsf{xsUpd}):=\mathsf{xsUpd}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\\
&(\mathsf{dz},\mathsf{seedUpd}):=\mathsf{seedUpd}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\\
&\textbf{for }\mathsf{j}=1..n\textbf{ do }\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt(\mathsf{dy}_{\mathsf{j}},\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{\mathsf{j}}):=\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{\mathsf{j}}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{\mathsf{j}}:=\mathsf{apply}(\mathsf{collections}_{\mathsf{j}},\mathsf{dy}_{\mathsf{j}});\\
&\mathsf{seeds}[1]:=\mathsf{apply}(\mathsf{seeds}[1],\mathsf{dz});\\
&\mathsf{toVisit}:=\{\mathsf{i}\mid(\mathsf{i},\_)\in\mathsf{dxs}.\textnormal{change}\};\\
&\textbf{for }\mathsf{j}=1..n\textbf{ do }\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{for each }(\mathsf{idx},{\,}\_)\!\in\mathsf{dy}_{\mathsf{j}}.\textnormal{change}\ \textbf{do}\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{toVisit}:=\mathsf{toVisit}\cup\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{idx}];\\
&\textbf{if }d\gamma\text{ changes any $\Gamma$-variable free in $t$ or any $u_{k}$}\textbf{ then }\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{toVisit}:=\{1,\dots,\mathsf{length}(\mathsf{inputs})\};\\
&\mathsf{changed}:=\emptyset;\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{i}:=1;\\
&\textbf{while }\mathsf{i}\leq\mathsf{length}(\mathsf{inputs})\textbf{ do }\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{if }\mathsf{isSame}(\mathsf{dz})\land\mathsf{i}\notin\mathsf{toVisit}\textbf{ then }\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\textbf{if }\mathsf{toVisit}=\emptyset\textbf{ then break};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{i}:=\text{pop min from }\mathsf{toVisit};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{dz}:=\mathsf{sameAs}(\mathsf{seeds}[\mathsf{i}]);\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{if }(\mathsf{i},\mathit{dx})\in\mathsf{dxs}.\textnormal{change}\textbf{ then }\mathsf{dx}:=\mathit{dx}\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{else }\mathsf{dx}:=\mathsf{sameAs}(\mathsf{inputs}[\mathsf{i}]);\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{for }\mathsf{j}=1..n\textbf{ do }\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt(\mathsf{didx}_{\mathsf{j}},\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{i}]):=\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{i}].\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}((d\gamma,\mathsf{dx},\mathsf{dz}));\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\textbf{if }\mathsf{isSame}(\mathsf{didx}_{\mathsf{j}})\textbf{ then }\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{idx}:=\mathsf{didx}_{\mathsf{j}}.\textnormal{new};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{if }(\mathsf{idx},\mathsf{dval})\in\mathsf{dy}_{\mathsf{j}}.\textnormal{change}\textbf{ then }\mathsf{dv}_{\mathsf{j}}:=\mathsf{dval}\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{else }\mathsf{dv}_{\mathsf{j}}:=\mathsf{sameAs}(\mathsf{collections}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{idx}])\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\textbf{else}\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{old}:=\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{i}];\ \mathsf{new}:=\mathsf{didx}_{\mathsf{j}}.\textnormal{new};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{i}]:=\mathsf{new};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{old}]:=\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{old}]\setminus\{\mathsf{i}\};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{new}]:=\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{new}]\cup\{\mathsf{i}\};\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{dv}_{\mathsf{j}}:=\text{change from }\mathsf{collections}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{old}]\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 17.00024pt\text{to }\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{\mathsf{j}}[\mathsf{new}]\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt((\mathsf{dy},\mathsf{dz}),\mathsf{updaters}[\mathsf{i}]):={}\\
&\hskip 17.00024pt\mathsf{updaters}[\mathsf{i}].\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}((d\gamma,\mathsf{dx},\mathsf{dz},\mathsf{dv}_{1},\dots,\mathsf{dv}_{n}));\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{seeds}[\mathsf{i}+1]:=\mathsf{apply}(\mathsf{seeds}[\mathsf{i}+1],\mathsf{dz});\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{inputs}[\mathsf{i}]:=\mathsf{apply}(\mathsf{inputs}[\mathsf{i}],\mathsf{dx});\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\textbf{if }\lnot\mathsf{isSame}(\mathsf{dy})\textbf{ then }\mathsf{changed}[\mathsf{i}]:=\mathsf{dy};\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{toVisit}:=\mathsf{toVisit}\setminus\{\mathsf{i}\};\\
&\hskip 8.50012pt\mathsf{i}:=\mathsf{i}+1;\\
&\mathsf{dresults}:=\{\textnormal{change:}\,\mathsf{changed}\};\mathsf{collections}:=\mathsf{collections}^{\prime};\\
&\mathsf{cache}.\textnormal{inputs}:=\mathsf{inputs};\,\dots;\,\mathsf{cache}.\textnormal{updaters}:=\mathsf{updaters};\\
&(\mathsf{dresults},\mathsf{cache})\end{aligned}

Figure 35
.
Incrementalizing transformation from
\lambda_{\mathsf{inc}}
to
\lambda_{\mathsf{core}}
on for-expressions.
For simplicity, the figure only handles modifications of list elements, not insertions or deletions, and we further assume that each collection expression
y_{j}
does not depend on the loop variable
x
or the accumulator
z
, so it can be typed in just
\Gamma
, matching the cache type declared for
\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\{(x,\mathit{dx},x^{\prime})\mid\mathit{dx}(\textnormal{new})=x^{\prime}\land(\mathit{dx}(\textnormal{same})=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} true}}}\implies x=x^{\prime})\}

\displaystyle\quad\text{ for }\sigma=\mathbb{B},\mathbb{N},\mathbb{R},\mathsf{Name}

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\times\tau}

\displaystyle=\{((x,y),(\mathit{dx},\mathit{dy}),(x^{\prime},y^{\prime}))\mid(x,\mathit{dx},x^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}\land(y,\mathit{dy},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{List}\;\sigma}

\displaystyle=\{(\mathit{xs},\{\textnormal{insert:}\,\mathit{is},\textnormal{remove:}\,\mathit{rs},\textnormal{change:}\,\mathit{cs}\},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\mid\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime}=(\mathit{xs}\text{ with all indices in }\mathit{rs}\text{ removed});

\displaystyle\qquad\phantom{{}\mid{}}\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime\prime}=(\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime}\text{ with changes in }\mathit{cs}\text{ applied}),

\displaystyle\qquad\phantom{{}\mid{}}\text{ i.e., for all }i,\begin{cases}(\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime}[i],\mathit{dx},\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime\prime}[i])\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}&\text{for }(i,\mathit{dx})\in\mathit{cs}\\
\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime\prime}[i]=\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime}[i]&\text{otherwise};\end{cases}

\displaystyle\qquad\phantom{{}\mid{}}\mathit{xs}^{\prime}=(\mathit{xs}^{\prime\prime\prime}\text{ with }x\text{ inserted at index $i$ for all }(i,x)\in\mathit{is})\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{NameMap}\;\sigma}

\displaystyle=\{(m,\{\textnormal{change:}\,\mathit{cm},\textnormal{remove:}\,\mathit{rm},\textnormal{add:}\,\mathit{am}\},m^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\mid\forall(n\mapsto\mathit{dv})\in\mathit{cm}\ldotp(m(n),\mathit{dv},m^{\prime}(n))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}

\displaystyle\qquad\land\forall(n\mapsto v)\in\mathit{am}\ldotp n\notin m\land m^{\prime}(n)=v

\displaystyle\qquad\land\forall n\in\mathit{rm}\ldotp n\in m\land n\notin m^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\land\forall n\in\mathsf{dom}(m)\setminus(\mathsf{dom}(\mathit{cm})\cup\mathit{rm})\ldotp n\in\mathsf{dom}(m^{\prime})\land m(n)=m^{\prime}(n)\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}}

\displaystyle=\{(r,\mathit{dr},r^{\prime})\mid\forall i\in\{1,\dots,n\}\ldotp\text{if }\ell_{i}\text{ in }\mathit{dr}\text{ then }(r(\ell_{i}),\mathit{dr}(\ell_{i}),r^{\prime}(\ell_{i}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau_{i}}\text{ else }r(\ell_{i})=r^{\prime}(\ell_{i})\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}

\displaystyle=\{((e,f),\mathit{de},(e^{\prime},f^{\prime}))\in\left\llbracket\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau\right\rrbracket\!\times\left\llbracket\Delta(\kappa)\right\rrbracket\!\times\left\llbracket\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau\right\rrbracket\!\mid(e,\mathit{de},e^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\kappa},f=f^{\prime}\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\Gamma}

\displaystyle=\{(\gamma,d\gamma,\gamma^{\prime})\in\left\llbracket\Gamma\right\rrbracket\!\times\left\llbracket\Delta(\Gamma)\right\rrbracket\!\times\left\llbracket\Gamma\right\rrbracket\!\mid\forall(x:\sigma)\in\Gamma\ldotp(\gamma(x),d\gamma(x),\gamma^{\prime}(x))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}\}

Figure 36
.
Relation of valid changes.

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\{(x,x^{\prime})\mid x=x^{\prime}\}\quad\text{ for ground types }\sigma

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\times\tau}

\displaystyle=\{((x,y),(x^{\prime},y^{\prime}))\mid(x,x^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}\land(y,y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{List}\;\sigma}

\displaystyle=\{(\mathit{xs},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mid\mathrm{length}(\mathit{xs})=\mathrm{length}(\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\land\forall i<\mathrm{length}(\mathit{xs})\ldotp(\mathit{xs}[i],\mathit{xs}^{\prime}[i])\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{NameMap}\;\sigma}

\displaystyle=\{(m,m^{\prime})\mid\mathrm{keys}(m)=\mathrm{keys}(m^{\prime})\land\forall n\in\mathrm{keys}(m)\ldotp(m(n),m^{\prime}(n))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}}

\displaystyle=\{(r,r^{\prime})\mid\forall i\in\{1,\dots,n\}\ldotp(r(\ell_{i}),r^{\prime}(\ell_{i}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau_{i}}\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}

\displaystyle=\{((\mathit{env},f),f^{\prime})\mid\forall(x,x^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}\ldotp(y,y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}\land u\in\mathcal{U}_{\kappa\times\sigma\leadsto\tau}^{f,(\mathit{env},x)}

\displaystyle\qquad\text{ where }y=f(\mathit{env},x),(y^{\prime},u)=f^{\prime}(x^{\prime})\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\Gamma}

\displaystyle=\{(\gamma,\gamma^{\prime})\mid\forall(x:\sigma)\in\Gamma\ldotp(\gamma(x),\gamma^{\prime}(x))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}\}

Figure 37
.
Logical relation between
\lambda_{\mathsf{inc}}
and
\lambda_{\mathsf{core}}
.

For this section, we use the context tuple conversion functions defined in
Fig.

18
.
We require some properties about contexts and context types.

Lemma C.1 (Properties of context types).

Let
\Gamma
be a context,
\gamma\in\left\llbracket\Gamma\right\rrbracket\!
.
Then for all
x:\sigma
in
\Gamma
, we have
\gamma(x)=\left\llbracket\pi_{\Gamma\mapsto x}\right\rrbracket\!(\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}(\gamma))
.
Similarly, for all
\mathit{xs}\subseteq\mathsf{FV}(t)
, we have
\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma|_{\mathit{xs}}}(\gamma|_{\mathit{xs}})=\left\llbracket\pi_{\Gamma\mapsto\mathit{xs}}\right\rrbracket\!(\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}(\gamma))
.
As a consequence, we have
(\gamma,\gamma^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\Gamma}
if and only if
(\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}(\gamma),\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)}
.
Furthermore,
\mathsf{ctx}_{\Gamma}
and
\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}
are inverses of each other.

Proof.

By induction on the structure of
\Gamma
.
∎

Lemma C.2 (Valid Updater Construction).

Let
f:\left\llbracket\tau\right\rrbracket\!\to\left\llbracket\tau^{\prime}\right\rrbracket\!
with cache type
\sigma
, and update function
u\in\left\llbracket\Delta(\tau)\times\sigma\to\Delta(\tau^{\prime})\times\sigma\right\rrbracket\!
.
For each
x\in\left\llbracket\tau\right\rrbracket\!
let
A_{x}\subseteq\left\llbracket\sigma\right\rrbracket\!
be a set (the “admissible caches at
x
”).
Assume “cache preservation” holds, i.e., for every valid change
(x,\mathit{dx},x^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}
and every
c\in A_{x}
, if

y=f(x),\quad y^{\prime}=f(x^{\prime}),\quad(\mathit{dy},c^{\prime})=u(\mathit{dx},c),

then
(y,\mathit{dy},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau^{\prime}}
and
c^{\prime}\in A_{x^{\prime}}
.
Then for any admissible initial cache
c\in A_{x}
, the updater built by
\mathrm{mkUpd}{c}{u}
(cf.
Fig.

26
) is valid for
f
at
x
:

\mathrm{mkUpd}(c,u)\in\mathcal{U}_{\tau\leadsto\tau^{\prime}}^{f,x}.

Proof.

Construct the set
U_{x}^{f}:=\{\mathrm{mkUpd}(c,u)\mid c\in A_{x}\}
of updaters built from caches in
A_{x}
.
Prove by definition of
\mathrm{mkUpd}
(
Fig.

26
) and by the assumption that for every valid change
(x,\mathit{dx},x^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}
and every
\mathrm{mkUpd}(c,u)\in U_{x}^{f}
, we have
(y,\mathit{dy},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau^{\prime}}
and
\mathrm{mkUpd}(c^{\prime},u)\in U_{x^{\prime}}^{f}
where
y=f(x),y^{\prime}=f(x^{\prime}),(\mathit{dy},c^{\prime})=u(\mathit{dx},c)
.
This proves that
U_{x}^{f}
is a post-fixpoint of the same operator used to define
\mathcal{U}_{\tau\leadsto\tau^{\prime}}^{f,x}
, and thus
U_{x}^{f}\subseteq\mathcal{U}_{\tau\leadsto\tau^{\prime}}^{f,x}
.
Thus
\mathrm{mkUpd}(c,u)\in U_{x}^{f}\subseteq\mathcal{U}_{\tau\leadsto\tau^{\prime}}^{f,x}
for any
c\in A_{x}
.
∎

We abbreviate
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}:=\pi_{1}\circ\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!
,
\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}:=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!\circ\mathsf{ctx}_{\Gamma}
, and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}:=\pi_{2}\circ\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!
.

Lemma C.3 (Fundamental Lemma).

Assume
\Gamma\vdash t:\tau
in
\lambda_{\mathsf{inc}}
, and thus
\mathcal{I}\!\left\{\Gamma\right\}\vdash\mathcal{I}_{\Gamma}\!\left\{t\right\}:\mathcal{I}\!\left\{\tau\right\}\times\mathsf{Upd}(\Delta(\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma));\Delta(\tau))
in
\lambda_{\mathsf{core}}
.
Then for all
(\gamma,\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{R}_{\Gamma}
, we have

(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}\quad\text{ and }\quad\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}(\gamma)}

Proof.

By induction on the typing derivation of
\Gamma\vdash t:\tau
.
Let
(\gamma,d\gamma,\gamma^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\Gamma}
be a valid context change and let
\hat{\gamma}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}(\gamma)
,
\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma}(\gamma^{\prime})
, and
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Delta(\Gamma)}(d\gamma)
be the corresponding tuple values.

High-level structure.

Every case follows the same two-step pattern.
First, the relational part is shown compositionally: using the induction hypotheses on subterms together with the appropriate clause of the logical relation, we derive

(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}
.
Second, for updater correctness we specify a family of admissible caches
A_{\hat{\gamma}}
and a single-step function
u
that threads the input change
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
through the cached sub-updaters, producing an output change and a new cache.
The only nontrivial check is cache preservation, which reduces to the induction hypotheses and the rules of the change relation; once this holds,
Lemma

C.2
yields

\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
We handle each case below.

Variables.

Let
(x:\tau)\in\Gamma
.
Then
\mathcal{I}\!\left\{x\right\}=(x,\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} mkUpd}}}(\{\};\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,\_)\ldotp(\pi_{\Delta(\Gamma)\mapsto x}\;d\gamma,\{\})))
.
So

(\left\llbracket x\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{x\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))=(\gamma(x),\gamma_{*}(x))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}

since
(\gamma,\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{R}_{\Gamma}
.

Updater correctness.
Let
u=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,\_)\ldotp(\pi_{\Delta(\Gamma)\mapsto x}\;d\gamma,\{\})
.
Then
y:=\left\llbracket x\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})=\left\llbracket x\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)=\gamma(x)
and
y^{\prime}:=\left\llbracket x\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})=\left\llbracket x\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})=\gamma^{\prime}(x)
.
Also
\mathit{dy}=\pi_{1}(\left\llbracket u\right\rrbracket\!(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},\{\}))=\left\llbracket\pi_{\Delta(\Gamma)\mapsto x}\right\rrbracket\!(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}})=d\gamma(x)
, where the last equality is
Lemma

C.1
applied to
\Delta(\Gamma)
and
d\gamma
.
Thus
(y,\mathit{dy},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}
by the assumption that
(\gamma,d\gamma,\gamma^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\Gamma}
.
By
Lemma

C.2
,
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{x\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})=\left\llbracket\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} mkUpd}}}(\{\};u)\right\rrbracket\!\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket x\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Constants.

The incrementalization of constants is proven on a case-by-case basis depending on the constant, typically using
Lemma

C.2
.

Let bindings.

Let
T=(x:=s;\,t)
with
\Gamma\vdash s:\sigma
and
\Gamma,x:\sigma\vdash t:\tau
.
Then by induction hypothesis, we have
(\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
Let
\gamma_{1}=\gamma[x\mapsto\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)]
and
\gamma_{1*}=\gamma_{*}[x\mapsto\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})]
be the extended contexts.
By the definition of the logical relation for contexts, we have
(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{1*})\in\mathcal{R}_{\Gamma,x:\sigma}
.
Thus by induction hypothesis again, we have
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1}),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{1*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{1*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma,x:\sigma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}_{1}}
where
\hat{\gamma}_{1}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma,x:\sigma}(\gamma_{1})=(\hat{\gamma},\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}))
.
The first part now follows from
\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1})
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{1*})
.

Updater correctness.
Let
u_{x}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})
and
u_{y}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{1*})
.
Then
y=\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})=\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1})
and
y^{\prime}=\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1}^{\prime})
where
\gamma_{1}^{\prime}=\gamma^{\prime}[x\mapsto\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})]
.
To apply
Lemma

C.2
, we define admissible caches

A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\{(u_{x},u_{y})\mid u_{x}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}},u_{y}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma,x:\sigma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},(\hat{\gamma},\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}))}\}

The cache update function is

u:=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,(u_{x},u_{y}))\ldotp(\mathit{dx},u_{x}^{\prime}):=u_{x}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);(\mathit{dy},u_{y}^{\prime}):=u_{y}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}((d\gamma,\mathit{dx}));(\mathit{dy},(u_{x}^{\prime},u_{y}^{\prime}))

Stepping
u_{x}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
yields
(\mathit{dv},u_{x}^{\prime})
with
(v,\mathit{dv},v^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}
and
u_{x}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
, where
v=\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)
and
v^{\prime}=\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})
.
Stepping
u_{y}
with
(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},\mathit{dv})
yields valid
(\mathit{dy},u_{y}^{\prime})
, i.e.
(y,\mathit{dy},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}
and
u_{y}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma,x:\sigma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}_{1}^{\prime}}
, where
y=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1})
and
y^{\prime}=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1}^{\prime})
.
Then by the definition of the semantics, we have
y=\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)
and
y^{\prime}=\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})
.
By unfolding, we find
\left\llbracket u\right\rrbracket\!(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},(u_{x},u_{y}))=(\mathit{dy},(u_{x}^{\prime},u_{y}^{\prime}))
.
Thus we have
(y,\mathit{dy},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}
and
(u_{x}^{\prime},u_{y}^{\prime})\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
By
Lemma

C.2
, we have
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})=\mathrm{mkUpd}((u_{x},u_{y}),u)\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Pairs.

Let
T=(s,t)
with
\Gamma\vdash s:\sigma
and
\Gamma\vdash t:\tau
.
By induction hypothesis, the translations of
s
and
t
are valid:
(\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}
and
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}
.
Since
\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)=(\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma))
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})=(\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}),\allowbreak\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))
, the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\times\tau}
implies
(\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\times\tau}
.

Updater correctness.
By induction hypothesis,
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
Define admissible caches

A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\{(u_{x},u_{y})\mid u_{x}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}},\,u_{y}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}\}

The cache update function is

u:=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,(u_{x},u_{y}))\ldotp(\mathit{dx},u_{x}^{\prime}):=u_{x}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\;(\mathit{dy},u_{y}^{\prime}):=u_{y}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\;((\mathit{dx},\mathit{dy}),(u_{x}^{\prime},u_{y}^{\prime}))

Stepping
u_{x}
and
u_{y}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
yields valid
(\mathit{dx},u_{x}^{\prime})
and
(\mathit{dy},u_{y}^{\prime})
, so we have
(\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\mathit{dx},\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}
,
u_{x}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
,
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\mathit{dy},\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}
, and
u_{y}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
As a consequence
(\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),(\mathit{dx},\mathit{dy}),\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\times\tau}
and
(u_{x}^{\prime},u_{y}^{\prime})\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
Finally,
Lemma

C.2
yields
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})=\mathrm{mkUpd}((u_{x},u_{y}),u)\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma\times\tau}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Projections.

Let
T=t.1
with
\Gamma\vdash t:\sigma\times\tau
.
By induction hypothesis,
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\times\tau}
.
By the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\times\tau}
, we have
(\pi_{1}(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)),\pi_{1}(\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}
.
Since
\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)=\pi_{1}(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma))
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})=\pi_{1}(\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))
, we obtain
(\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}
.

Updater correctness.
Let
u_{t}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})
.
Define
A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma\times\tau}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
and

u:=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,u_{t})\ldotp(\mathit{dx},u_{t}^{\prime}):=u_{t}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\;(\mathit{dx}.1,u_{t}^{\prime})

Stepping
u_{t}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
yields valid
(\mathit{dx},u_{t}^{\prime})
, i.e.
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\mathit{dx},\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\times\tau}
and
u_{t}^{\prime}\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
By the definition of
\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\times\tau}
, we have
(\pi_{1}(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)),\pi_{1}(\mathit{dx}),\pi_{1}(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}
.
By
Lemma

C.2
, it follows that
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
The case
T=t.2
is symmetric.

Abstractions.

Let
T=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}x\ldotp t
with
\Gamma,x:\sigma\vdash t:\tau
, so
T:\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau
where
\kappa=\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)})
.
Then we have
\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}=(\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}x\ldotp\mathcal{I}_{\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)},x:\sigma}\!\left\{t\right\},\,\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} mkUpd}}}(\{\};\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,\_)\ldotp(\pi_{\Delta(\Gamma)\mapsto\mathsf{FV}(T)}\;d\gamma,\{\})))
.
Let
\mathit{env}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)}}(\gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)})
and
f=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)},x:\sigma}}
, so that
\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)=(\mathit{env},f)
.
Let
f^{\prime}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathcal{I}_{\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)},x:\sigma}\!\left\{t\right\}(\gamma_{*}|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)},x^{\prime})
.
To show
(\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}
, consider any
(v,v^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}
.
Let
\gamma_{1}=\gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)}[x\mapsto v]
and
\gamma_{1*}=\gamma_{*}|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)}[x\mapsto v^{\prime}]
.
Since
(\gamma,\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{R}_{\Gamma}
, we have
(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{1*})\in\mathcal{R}_{\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)},x:\sigma}
.
By induction hypothesis on
t
in context
\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)},x:\sigma
:

(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1}),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{1*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}\quad\text{ and }\quad\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{1*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\kappa\times\sigma\leadsto\tau}^{f,(\mathit{env},v)}

Since
f(\mathit{env},v)=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{1})
and
f^{\prime}(v^{\prime})=(\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{1*}),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{1*}))
, this is exactly what the closure logical relation requires.

Updater correctness.
Let
u_{\lambda}=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,\_)\ldotp(\pi_{\Delta(\Gamma)\mapsto\mathsf{FV}(T)}\;d\gamma,\{\})
.
Define
A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\{\{\}\}
(a singleton).
Then
\mathit{dy}=\pi_{\Delta(\Gamma)\mapsto\mathsf{FV}(T)}\;\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
, which is the projection of the context change to the free variables of
T
.
By
Lemma

C.1
,
(\mathit{env},\mathit{dy},\mathit{env}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\kappa}
where
\mathit{env}^{\prime}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)}}(\gamma^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{FV}(T)})
.
Since
\Delta(\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau)=\Delta(\kappa)
, and the underlying function
f
is the same before and after the change (only the captured environment changes), we have
(\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\mathit{dy},\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}
.
The cache is
\{\}\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
trivially.
By
Lemma

C.2
,
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})=\mathrm{mkUpd}(\{\},u_{\lambda})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Application.

Let
T=s\;t
with
\Gamma\vdash s:\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau
and
\Gamma\vdash t:\sigma
.
By induction hypothesis on
s
, we have
(\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
By induction hypothesis on
t
, we have
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
Let
(\mathit{env},f)=\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)
,
f^{\prime}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})
,
x=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)
, and
x^{\prime}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})
; by the IH on
t
above,
(x,x^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\sigma}
.
Set
y=f(\mathit{env},x)
and
(y^{\prime},u_{y})=f^{\prime}(x^{\prime})
.
By the closure logical relation, we have
(y,y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau}
and
u_{y}\in\mathcal{U}_{\kappa\times\sigma\leadsto\tau}^{f,(\mathit{env},x)}
.
Since
\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)=y
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})=y^{\prime}
, the relational part follows.

Updater correctness.
Let
u_{f}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})
and
u_{x}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})
.
Define admissible caches

\displaystyle A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\{(u_{f},u_{x},u_{y})

\displaystyle\mid u_{f}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}},u_{x}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}},u_{y}\in\mathcal{U}_{\kappa\times\sigma\leadsto\tau}^{f,(\mathit{env},x)}

\displaystyle\quad\text{ where }(\mathit{env},f)=\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}),x=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})\}

The cache update function is

\displaystyle u

\displaystyle:=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} $\bm{\lambda}$}}}(d\gamma,(u_{f},u_{x},u_{y}))\ldotp(\mathit{df},u_{f}^{\prime}):=u_{f}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\,(\mathit{dx},u_{x}^{\prime}):=u_{x}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}(d\gamma);\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\,(\mathit{dy},u_{y}^{\prime}):=u_{y}.\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} apply}}}((\mathit{df},\mathit{dx}));\,(\mathit{dy},(u_{f}^{\prime},u_{x}^{\prime},u_{y}^{\prime}))

Stepping
u_{f}
and
u_{x}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
yields valid
(\mathit{df},u_{f}^{\prime})
and
(\mathit{dx},u_{x}^{\prime})
, i.e.
(\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\mathit{df},\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}
,
u_{f}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
,
(x,\mathit{dx},x^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\sigma}
, and
u_{x}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\sigma}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
, where
x^{\prime}=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})
.
Let
(\mathit{env}^{\prime},f^{\prime})=\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})
.
By the definition of
\mathcal{C}_{\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau}
(recalling
\Delta(\sigma\to_{\kappa}\tau)=\Delta(\kappa)
), the validity of
\mathit{df}
above unpacks to
(\mathit{env},\mathit{df},\mathit{env}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\kappa}
together with
f^{\prime}=f
.
Hence
((\mathit{env},x),(\mathit{df},\mathit{dx}),(\mathit{env}^{\prime},x^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\kappa\times\sigma}
.
Stepping
u_{y}
with
(\mathit{df},\mathit{dx})
yields valid
(\mathit{dy},u_{y}^{\prime})
, i.e.
(y,\mathit{dy},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau}
and
u_{y}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\kappa\times\sigma\leadsto\tau}^{f,(\mathit{env}^{\prime},x^{\prime})}
.
Since
f^{\prime}=f
, the function component named in the definition of
A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
(extracted from
\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})
) is again
f
, so
(u_{f}^{\prime},u_{x}^{\prime},u_{y}^{\prime})\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
By
Lemma

C.2
, it follows that
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})=\mathrm{mkUpd}((u_{f},u_{x},u_{y}),u)\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Record literals.

Record literals
T=\{\ell_{1}:t_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:t_{n}\}
are the
n
-ary generalization of pairs.
(Note, however, that their change types differ from those of pairs: changes to records are subrecords, which may omit unchanged fields.)
The relational part follows from the induction hypothesis on each
t_{i}
and the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}}
.
For updater correctness, the admissible caches are
A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau_{i}}^{\left\llbracket t_{i}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
, and the cache update function threads
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
through each sub-updater independently.
Cache preservation follows from the validity of each sub-updater and the definition of
\mathcal{C}_{\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}}
, using
Lemma

C.2
.

Record projection.

Let
T=t.\ell_{j}
with
\Gamma\vdash t:\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}
and result type
\tau_{j}
.
By induction hypothesis,
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}}
and
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\{\dots\}}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
By the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\{\dots\}}
, the
\ell_{j}
-component is related:
(\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau_{j}}
.

Updater correctness.
We distinguish two sub-cases depending on whether
\mathsf{same}_{\tau_{j}}
exists.

Case 1:
\mathsf{same}_{\tau_{j}}
exists.

The cache is
u_{r}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})
, so define
A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\{\dots\}}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
The cache update function steps
u_{r}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
to obtain
(\mathit{dr},u_{r}^{\prime})
.
If
\ell_{j}
is present in
\mathit{dr}
, the output is
\mathit{dr}(\ell_{j})
; otherwise it is
\left\llbracket\mathsf{same}_{\tau_{j}}\right\rrbracket\!
.
For cache preservation, let
(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},u_{r})\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)}\times A_{\hat{\gamma}}
.
By validity of
u_{r}
, stepping it with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
yields
(\mathit{dr},u_{r}^{\prime})
with
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}),\mathit{dr},\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\{\dots\}}
and
u_{r}^{\prime}\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
If
\ell_{j}\in\mathit{dr}
: by
\mathcal{C}_{\{\dots\}}
,
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})(\ell_{j}),\mathit{dr}(\ell_{j}),\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})(\ell_{j}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau_{j}}
, so the output change is valid.
If
\ell_{j}\notin\mathit{dr}
: by
\mathcal{C}_{\{\dots\}}
,
\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})(\ell_{j})=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})(\ell_{j})
, so
\left\llbracket\mathsf{same}_{\tau_{j}}\right\rrbracket\!
is a valid change.
By
Lemma

C.2
,
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau_{j}}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Case 2:
\mathsf{same}_{\tau_{j}}
does not exist (but
\mathsf{sameAs}_{\tau_{j}}
and
\mathsf{apply}_{\tau_{j}}
exist).

The cache is
(r_{\ell},u_{r})
where
r_{\ell}=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})(\ell_{j})
and
u_{r}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})
.
Define

A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\{(r_{\ell},u_{r})\mid u_{r}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\{\dots\}}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}\;\land\;r_{\ell}=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})(\ell_{j})\}

The cache update function steps
u_{r}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
to obtain
(\mathit{dr},u_{r}^{\prime})
.
If
\ell_{j}\in\mathit{dr}
: the output is
\mathit{dr}(\ell_{j})
and the new cache is
(\left\llbracket\mathsf{apply}_{\tau_{j}}\right\rrbracket\!(r_{\ell},\mathit{dr}(\ell_{j})),\;u_{r}^{\prime})
.
If
\ell_{j}\notin\mathit{dr}
: the output is
\left\llbracket\mathsf{sameAs}_{\tau_{j}}\right\rrbracket\!(r_{\ell})
and the new cache is
(r_{\ell},u_{r}^{\prime})
.
For cache preservation, let
(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},(r_{\ell},u_{r}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)}\times A_{\hat{\gamma}}
, so
r_{\ell}=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})(\ell_{j})
.
By validity of
u_{r}
, stepping it yields
(\mathit{dr},u_{r}^{\prime})
with
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}),\mathit{dr},\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\{\dots\}}
and
u_{r}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\{\dots\}}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
If
\ell_{j}\in\mathit{dr}
: by
\mathcal{C}_{\{\dots\}}
,
(r_{\ell},\mathit{dr}(\ell_{j}),\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})(\ell_{j}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau_{j}}
, so the output change is valid.
The new cached value
\left\llbracket\mathsf{apply}_{\tau_{j}}\right\rrbracket\!(r_{\ell},\mathit{dr}(\ell_{j}))=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})(\ell_{j})
, so the new cache is in
A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
If
\ell_{j}\notin\mathit{dr}
: by
\mathcal{C}_{\{\dots\}}
,
r_{\ell}=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})(\ell_{j})=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})(\ell_{j})
, so
\left\llbracket\mathsf{sameAs}_{\tau_{j}}\right\rrbracket\!(r_{\ell})
is a valid change and
(r_{\ell},u_{r}^{\prime})\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
By
Lemma

C.2
,
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\tau_{j}}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Record restriction.

Let
T=t|_{\{\ell_{i_{1}},\dots,\ell_{i_{k}}\}}
with
\Gamma\vdash t:\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}
and result type
\{\ell_{i_{1}}:\tau_{i_{1}},\dots,\ell_{i_{k}}:\tau_{i_{k}}\}
.
The relational part follows from the induction hypothesis on
t
and the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\{\dots\}}
.
For updater correctness, the cache is
u_{r}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})
, so define
A_{\hat{\gamma}}:=\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\{\dots\}}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.
The cache update function steps
u_{r}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
to obtain
(\mathit{dr},u_{r}^{\prime})
and outputs
\mathit{dr}|_{\{\ell_{i_{1}},\dots,\ell_{i_{k}}\}}
.
For cache preservation: by validity of
u_{r}
, we get
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}),\mathit{dr},\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\{\ell_{1}:\tau_{1},\dots,\ell_{n}:\tau_{n}\}}
and
u_{r}^{\prime}\in A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
By
\mathcal{C}_{\{\dots\}}
, for each
\ell_{i_{p}}
: if
\ell_{i_{p}}\in\mathit{dr}
then
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})(\ell_{i_{p}}),\mathit{dr}(\ell_{i_{p}}),\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})(\ell_{i_{p}}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau_{i_{p}}}
, and if
\ell_{i_{p}}\notin\mathit{dr}
then the field is unchanged.
This is exactly what
\mathcal{C}_{\{\ell_{i_{1}}:\tau_{i_{1}},\dots,\ell_{i_{k}}:\tau_{i_{k}}\}}
requires for the restricted change, so the output change is valid.
By
Lemma

C.2
,
\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{2}(\gamma_{*})\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\{\ell_{i_{1}}:\tau_{i_{1}},\dots,\ell_{i_{k}}:\tau_{i_{k}}\}}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

For loops (list version).

Let
T=\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} for}}}\;x\;\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} in}}}\;\mathit{xs}\;\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} with}}}\;z:=s\;\textnormal{{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0.5} using}}}\;(v_{1}:=y_{1}[u_{1}],\dots,v_{n}:=y_{n}[u_{n}])\;\{\;t\;\}
with
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{xs}:\mathsf{List}\;\tau
,
\Gamma\vdash s:\rho
,
\Gamma,x:\tau,z:\rho\vdash y_{j}[u_{j}]:\theta_{j}
for
j=1,\dots,n
, and
\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}\vdash t:\tau^{\prime}\times\rho
where
\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}=\Gamma,x:\tau,z:\rho,v_{1}:\theta_{1},\dots,v_{n}:\theta_{n}
; the result type is
\mathsf{List}\;\tau^{\prime}
.
Let
(a_{1},\dots,a_{N})=\left\llbracket\mathit{xs}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)
be the input list of length
N
, and let
z_{0}=\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)
.
For each
i=1,\dots,N
, let
\gamma_{i}^{0}=\gamma[x\mapsto a_{i},z\mapsto z_{i-1}]
, the lookup values
w_{j}=\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0})[\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0})]
for
j=1,\dots,n
(typed in
\Gamma,x:\tau,z:\rho
— they do not see prior
v
-bindings), and the extended context
\gamma_{i}=\gamma_{i}^{0}[v_{1}\mapsto w_{1},\dots,v_{n}\mapsto w_{n}]
.
Let
(r_{i},z_{i})=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i})
be the per-iteration result and updated accumulator.
Define the analogous incrementalized contexts: let
(a_{1}^{\prime},\dots,a_{N}^{\prime})=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{\mathit{xs}\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})
,
z_{0}^{\prime}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*})
,
\gamma_{i*}^{0}=\gamma_{*}[x\mapsto a_{i}^{\prime},z\mapsto z_{i-1}^{\prime}]
,
w_{j}^{\prime}=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{y_{j}\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{i*}^{0})[\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{u_{j}\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{i*}^{0})]
,
\gamma_{i*}=\gamma_{i*}^{0}[v_{1}\mapsto w_{1}^{\prime},\dots,v_{n}\mapsto w_{n}^{\prime}]
, and
z_{i}^{\prime}=\pi_{2}(\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{i*}))
.

For the relational part, we have
(\left\llbracket\mathit{xs}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{\mathit{xs}\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{List}\;\tau}
and
(\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{s\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\rho}
by induction hypothesis applied to
\mathit{xs}
and
s
.
For each iteration
i
(by induction on
i
, since
\gamma_{i*}
depends recursively on
z_{i-1}^{\prime}
), the induction hypotheses on
u_{j}
,
y_{j}
, and
t
yield related values for the index computations, collection lookups, and body evaluation, from which
(\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{i*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau^{\prime}\times\rho}
.
Projecting to the first component gives the per-iteration element relation
(r_{i},r_{i}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{R}_{\tau^{\prime}}
, where
(r_{i},z_{i})=\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i})
and
(r_{i}^{\prime},z_{i}^{\prime})=\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{t\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{i*})
.
Since the logical relation for lists requires element-wise relatedness, we obtain
(\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\left\llbracket\mathcal{I}\!\left\{T\right\}\right\rrbracket\!_{1}(\gamma_{*}))\in\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{List}\;\tau^{\prime}}
.

Updater correctness.
The incrementalizing transformation for for-expressions (
Fig.

35
) constructs a cache containing the following components:
the input list
\mathsf{xs}
and its updater
\mathsf{xsUpd}
, the sequence of accumulated values
\mathsf{seeds}=[z_{0},z_{1},\dots,z_{N}]
(stored 1-indexed so that
\mathsf{seeds}[i]=z_{i-1}
), the seed updater
\mathsf{seedUpd}
, the per-iteration body updaters
\mathsf{updaters}=[u_{1}^{\mathit{body}},\dots,u_{N}^{\mathit{body}}]
, the collection values and their updaters
\mathsf{collections}_{j}
and
\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}
for
j=1,\dots,n
, the per-iteration index updaters
\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{j}[i]
for each lookup
j
and iteration
i
, and the index tracking maps
\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{j}
and
\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{j}
.
Let
\hat{\gamma}_{i}^{\mathit{ext}}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}}(\gamma_{i})
be the tuple form of the per-iteration extended context.

Define admissible caches
A_{\hat{\gamma}}
as the set of caches satisfying:

(1)

\mathsf{seeds}[i]=z_{i-1}
for
i=1,\dots,N+1
;

(2)

\mathsf{seedUpd}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\rho}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
;

(3)

u_{i}^{\mathit{body}}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}})\leadsto\tau^{\prime}\times\rho}^{\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}}},\hat{\gamma}_{i}^{\mathit{ext}}}
for
i=1,\dots,N
(note: the underlying function
\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}}}
is the same across all iterations; only the input point
\hat{\gamma}_{i}^{\mathit{ext}}
varies);

(4)

\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{j}[i]\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}((\Gamma,x:\tau,z:\rho))\leadsto\mathsf{Idx}_{j}}^{\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma,x:\tau,z:\rho}},\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma,x:\tau,z:\rho}(\gamma_{i}^{0})}
for each
j
and
i
, where
\mathsf{Idx}_{j}
is
\mathbb{N}
or
\mathsf{Name}
depending on whether
y_{j}
is a list or a name map;

(5)

\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{j}}^{\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
for each
j
;

(6)

the index maps
\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{j}
and
\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{j}
are consistent with the current index values, i.e.,
\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{j}[i]=\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0})
and
\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{j}[\mathsf{idx}]=\{i\mid\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{j}[i]=\mathsf{idx}\}
;

(7)

\mathsf{xsUpd}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\mathsf{List}\;\tau}^{\left\llbracket\mathit{xs}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
and
\mathsf{collections}_{j}=\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma})
for each
j
.

The cache update function (
Fig.

35
, right column) processes a context change
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
as follows.
First, it steps the input-list updater
\mathsf{xsUpd}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
to obtain
(\mathit{dxs},\mathsf{xsUpd}^{\prime})
, and the seed updater
\mathsf{seedUpd}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
to obtain
(\mathit{dz}_{0},\mathsf{seedUpd}^{\prime})
, where
\mathit{dz}_{0}
is the change to the initial accumulator
z_{0}
.
Next, it steps each collection updater
\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}
with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
to obtain
(\mathit{dy}_{j},\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}^{\prime})
and computes the post-change collection
\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{j}:=\mathsf{apply}(\mathsf{collections}_{j},\mathit{dy}_{j})
, keeping the pre-change
\mathsf{collections}_{j}
available for change construction inside the per-iteration loop.
It then determines the set
\mathsf{toVisit}
of iterations that need re-evaluation: this includes iterations whose input element changed (from
\mathit{dxs}.\textnormal{change}
) and iterations referencing changed collection entries (identified via
\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{j}[\mathsf{idx}]
for each
\mathsf{idx}
with
(\mathsf{idx},\_)\in\mathit{dy}_{j}.\textnormal{change}
). In addition, if
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
changes any
\Gamma
-variable free in
t
or in any
u_{j}
, then
\mathsf{toVisit}
is set to all iterations
\{1,\dots,N\}
(these are the variables whose changes are not already captured by per-iteration mechanisms).

Then the iterations are processed, threading a running accumulator change
\mathit{dz}
(initialized to
\mathit{dz}_{0}
). The cursor
i
starts at
1
and, after each processed iteration,
i
is removed from
\mathsf{toVisit}
and incremented. At each step:

•

If
\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{dz})
and
i\notin\mathsf{toVisit}
, the algorithm jumps ahead: if
\mathsf{toVisit}
is empty it breaks out of the loop; otherwise it pops the smallest element of
\mathsf{toVisit}
as the new
i
and sets
\mathit{dz}:=\mathsf{sameAs}(\mathsf{seeds}[i])
. Since the invariant “
\mathsf{toVisit}
contains only iterations not yet processed” is maintained by the post-iteration removal, the popped value is always strictly greater than the previous
i
, and the algorithm falls through to process iteration
i
.

•

To process iteration
i
, the per-iteration index updaters
\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{j}[i]
are stepped with the change
(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},\mathit{dx}_{i},\mathit{dz})
at the lookup point
(\hat{\gamma},a_{i},z_{i-1})
, yielding a valid index change
\mathit{didx}_{j}
. If
\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{didx}_{j})
, the index expression evaluates to the same index
\mathsf{idx}=\mathit{didx}_{j}.\textnormal{new}
as before; we then set
\mathit{dv}_{j}:=\mathit{dval}
if
(\mathsf{idx},\mathit{dval})\in\mathit{dy}_{j}.\textnormal{change}
, and otherwise set
\mathit{dv}_{j}:=\mathsf{sameAs}(\mathsf{collections}_{j}[\mathsf{idx}])
. If
\lnot\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{didx}_{j})
, the index maps are updated and
\mathit{dv}_{j}
is constructed as the change from the pre-change value
\mathsf{collections}_{j}[\mathsf{old}]
to the post-change value
\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{j}[\mathsf{new}]
. Then the per-iteration body updater
u_{i}^{\mathit{body}}
is stepped with the input change
(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},\mathit{dx}_{i},\mathit{dz},\mathit{dv}_{1},\dots,\mathit{dv}_{n})
to obtain the output change
(\mathit{dr}_{i},\mathit{dz}_{i})
and a new updater
u_{i}^{\mathit{body}\prime}
. (Here
\mathit{dx}_{i}
is the element change at position
i
, taken from
\mathit{dxs}.\textnormal{change}
if present and
\mathsf{sameAs}(a_{i})
otherwise.) The accumulator change
\mathit{dz}
is updated to
\mathit{dz}_{i}
and propagated to the next iteration.

At the end, the cache’s
\mathsf{collections}_{j}
is replaced by the post-change
\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{j}
.

Cache preservation. We need to show that the updated cache lies in
A_{\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
.
By validity of
\mathsf{seedUpd}
, stepping it with
\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}}
yields
(\mathit{dz}_{0},\mathsf{seedUpd}^{\prime})
with
(z_{0},\mathit{dz}_{0},z_{0}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\rho}
where
z_{0}^{\prime}=\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})
, and also
\mathsf{seedUpd}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\rho}^{\left\llbracket s\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
, so clause 2 of the admissible-cache definition is preserved.
By validity of each
\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}
, stepping it yields
(\mathit{dy}_{j},\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}^{\prime})
with
(\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\mathit{dy}_{j},\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{j}}
and
\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\mathsf{Coll}\;\theta_{j}}^{\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
, so clause 5 is preserved.
By the validity of
\mathsf{xsUpd}
, stepping it yields
(\mathit{dxs},\mathsf{xsUpd}^{\prime})
with
(\left\llbracket\mathit{xs}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma),\mathit{dxs},\left\llbracket\mathit{xs}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{List}\;\tau}
as well as
\mathsf{xsUpd}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\mathsf{List}\;\tau}^{\left\llbracket\mathit{xs}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}}
, so clause 7 is preserved.
Furthermore,
\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{j}=\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}}(\hat{\gamma}^{\prime})
holds by validity of
\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}
, justifying the cache’s
\mathsf{collections}_{j}
being replaced by
\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{j}
at the end of the update.

The remaining clauses (1, 3, 4, 6) are preserved by induction on
i=1,\dots,N
, maintaining as invariant that
(z_{i-1},\mathit{dz},z_{i-1}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\rho}
when entering iteration
i
. Let
\hat{\gamma}_{i}^{\mathit{ext}\prime}=\mathsf{tpl}_{\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}}(\gamma_{i}^{\prime})
where
\gamma_{i}^{\prime}
is the post-change per-iteration context (defined analogously to
\gamma_{i}
but using
\gamma^{\prime}
and the post-change lookups).

For an iteration
i
with
i\in\mathsf{toVisit}
or
\lnot\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{dz})
: by validity of
\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{j}[i]
(clause 4), stepping it with the input change
(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},\mathit{dx}_{i},\mathit{dz})
— which lies in
\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{CtxType}((\Gamma,x:\tau,z:\rho))}
at the lookup point
(\hat{\gamma},a_{i},z_{i-1})
— yields a valid index change
\mathit{didx}_{j}
. If
\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{didx}_{j})
, set
\mathsf{idx}=\mathit{didx}_{j}.\textnormal{new}
; the constructed
\mathit{dv}_{j}
is either
\mathit{dval}
where
(\mathsf{idx},\mathit{dval})\in\mathit{dy}_{j}.\textnormal{change}
(which is a valid entry change by validity of
\mathsf{collectionUpds}_{j}
) or
\mathsf{sameAs}(\mathsf{collections}_{j}[\mathsf{idx}])
(which is valid because absence of an entry of
\mathit{dy}_{j}.\textnormal{change}
at
\mathsf{idx}
means the entry is unchanged). If
\lnot\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{didx}_{j})
, the constructed
\mathit{dv}_{j}
is the change from
\mathsf{collections}_{j}[\mathsf{old}]=\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma)[\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0})]
to
\mathsf{collections}^{\prime}_{j}[\mathsf{new}]=\left\llbracket y_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma^{\prime})[\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0\prime})]
, which is exactly the change to
v_{j}
between
\gamma_{i}
and
\gamma_{i}^{\prime}
. Index-map consistency (clause 6) at
i
holds in both sub-cases: if
\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{didx}_{j})
the index is unchanged, and otherwise the explicit updates to
\mathsf{itersToIndices}_{j}
and
\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{j}
restore consistency.

Then
(\mathit{d\hat{\gamma}},\mathit{dx}_{i},\mathit{dz},\mathit{dv}_{1},\dots,\mathit{dv}_{n})
is a valid change in
\mathcal{C}_{\Gamma_{\mathit{ext}}}
at
\hat{\gamma}_{i}^{\mathit{ext}}
, so by validity of
u_{i}^{\mathit{body}}
, stepping it produces
(\mathit{dr}_{i},\mathit{dz}_{i})
with
(r_{i},\mathit{dr}_{i},r_{i}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau^{\prime}}
and
(z_{i},\mathit{dz}_{i},z_{i}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{C}_{\rho}
, and the new updater
u_{i}^{\mathit{body}\prime}
is valid at
\hat{\gamma}_{i}^{\mathit{ext}\prime}
.

For an iteration
i
with
\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{dz})
and
i\notin\mathsf{toVisit}
: the algorithm skips iteration
i
, so we must show the cache entries at index
i
remain valid at
\hat{\gamma}^{\prime}
without being updated. The
\mathsf{toVisit}
conditions give
a_{i}=a_{i}^{\prime}
, no
\Gamma
-variable free in
t
or any
u_{j}
has changed (else
\mathsf{toVisit}=\{1,\dots,N\}
), and
(\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0}),\_)\notin\mathit{dy}_{j}.\textnormal{change}
for each
j
(else
i
would be in
\mathsf{toVisit}
via
\mathsf{indicesToIters}_{j}
). Together with
z_{i-1}=z_{i-1}^{\prime}
from
\mathsf{isSame}(\mathit{dz})
, these yield
\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0})=\left\llbracket u_{j}\right\rrbracket\!(\gamma_{i}^{0\prime})
and
w_{j}=w_{j}^{\prime}
for each
j
, hence
\gamma_{i}^{\mathit{ext}}
and
\gamma_{i}^{\mathit{ext}\prime}
agree on the free variables of
t
(and analogously
\gamma_{i}^{0}
and
\gamma_{i}^{0\prime}
agree on those of each
u_{j}
), so
(r_{i},z_{i})=(r_{i}^{\prime},z_{i}^{\prime})
by functionality of
\left\llbracket t\right\rrbracket\!
. The unchanged
\mathsf{seeds}[i+1]
,
u_{i}^{\mathit{body}}
,
\mathsf{idxUpdaters}_{j}[i]
, and index-map entries at
i
therefore still satisfy clauses 1, 3, 4, and 6. When the algorithm later pops some
k
and sets
\mathit{dz}:=\mathsf{sameAs}(\mathsf{seeds}[k])=\mathsf{sameAs}(z_{k-1})
, chaining
z_{m}=z_{m}^{\prime}
across the skipped indices between the previous processed/popped index and
k-1
gives
z_{k-1}=z_{k-1}^{\prime}
, so the assignment is a valid change in
\mathcal{C}_{\rho}
and re-establishes the running-
\mathit{dz}
invariant for iteration
k
.

The output
\mathsf{dresults}=\{\textnormal{change:}\,\mathsf{changed}\}
records exactly the iterations whose body output element actually changed, which is a valid change in
\mathcal{C}_{\mathsf{List}\;\tau^{\prime}}
between
(r_{1},\dots,r_{N})
and
(r_{1}^{\prime},\dots,r_{N}^{\prime})
.
By
Lemma

C.2
, the constructed updater is in
\mathcal{U}_{\mathsf{CtxType}(\Gamma)\leadsto\mathsf{List}\;\tau^{\prime}}^{\left\llbracket T\right\rrbracket\!_{\mathsf{tpl}},\hat{\gamma}}
.

Note.

The pseudocode in
Fig.

35
only handles modifications to list elements, not insertions or deletions, in order to keep the presentation readable. We also assume each collection expression
y_{j}
does not depend on
x
or
z
, so
\mathcal{I}\!\left\{y_{j}\right\}
produces the same value on every iteration of the outer loop (otherwise the cached
\mathsf{collections}_{j}
would need to be per-iteration, complicating the bookkeeping).
The for-expression over name sets is analogous, with
\mathsf{NameMap}\;\tau^{\prime}
in place of
\mathsf{List}\;\tau^{\prime}
, iteration over names (with the loop variable bound to a
\mathsf{Name}
) in place of iteration over a list (with the loop variable bound to an element), and without an accumulator (so
\mathsf{seeds}
and the body’s accumulator output drop out of the cache and the body’s value type is just
\tau^{\prime}
rather than
\tau^{\prime}\times\rho
).
Our implementation also handles insertions and deletions for name collections, but not for lists (since this wasn’t needed for the benchmarks).

∎
